## Best date plans

### 2026-09-10 — Superiority Burger + The Incomer: Sneak Preview with Domhnall Gleeson Intro

**Dinner, then The Incomer: Sneak Preview with Domhnall Gleeson Intro.** Vegetarian diner at Superiority Burger. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. The Incomer: Sneak Preview with Domhnall Gleeson Intro starts at 8:45 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=119+Avenue+A%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&destination=323+6th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.ifccenter.com/films/the-incomer/) · [Event booking](https://tickets.ifccenter.com/websales/pages/ticketsearchcriteria.aspx?evtinfo=578638~8722a542-64c1-4c0b-af02-c3e8a239d769&) · [Restaurant](https://www.superiorityburger.com/)

## Where to eat

Verified current NYC dining leads for prices, dates, openings, duplicate offers and source-backed conditions as of 2026-09-07.

Dining research: 2026-09-07T10:38:11+00:00

No candidate is verified here as holding a Michelin star. Grand Street BID copy describes Ammazzacaffè as a Michelin Bib Gourmand recipient, which is not a star and was not added as an award claim. Eater describes the Mexico City original of El Califa de León as Michelin-starred, but the aggregate Manhattan heatmap card was rejected rather than expanded into unsupported individual listings.

### Hawksmoor New York lunch special: 1/2/3 courses for $25/$40/$55

Deals & happy hours — Hawksmoor New York

A useful official weekday-and-Saturday lunch set menu near Gramercy with steakhouse mains such as fish and chips, steak frites, and seasonal starters.

Price: 1 course $25; 2 courses $40; 3 courses $55

#### [64th New York Film Festival tickets on sale](https://www.filmlinc.org/nyff/)

2026-09-15 · 12:00 PM · film · Pricing subject to change · Film at Lincoln Center · Lincoln Square · Manhattan · Score 65.0  
NYFF64 returns with screenings across Film at Lincoln Center venues; the public single-ticket sale opens September 15 at noon ET, after member and passholder presales.
**Timing:** Festival runs September 25-October 12, 2026; single tickets go on sale September 15 at noon ET. Date re-verified against the venue: moved from 2026-09-25 to 2026-09-15.
**Practical note:** Plan a ranked shortlist before noon; Alice Tully Hall, Walter Reade Theater, and EBM screenings have a two-ticket maximum per screening.
**Why now:** The general-public ticket release is a dated future on-sale inside the book-ahead window.
**Book soon:** Film at Lincoln Center states that NYFF64 single tickets go on sale Tuesday, September 15 at 12pm ET, with earlier access for passholders and members.
